<script>
(function () {
    var t = null;
    try { t = localStorage.getItem('theme'); } catch (e) {}
    if (t !== 'light' && t !== 'dark') t = 'light';
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', t);
    /* Marks this page as being on the token system. Shared partials key
       their token-aware skins on html.theme-v2 so legacy pages that
       include the same partials are untouched. */
    document.documentElement.classList.add('theme-v2');
    /* The plain look (design_briefs/00_PRINCIPLES.md). A page opts in by setting the
       template variable look_plain to true before this include (the Scout Hub shell does).
       A choice made in this browser wins over the page: ?look=old keeps the earlier look
       everywhere, ?look=plain keeps the new look everywhere, ?look=auto clears the choice.
       Decided here in the browser because pages are edge-cached. */
    var lp = null;
    try {
        var m = /[?&]look=(plain|old|auto|off)(&|$)/.exec(location.search);
        if (m) { if (m[1] === 'plain' || m[1] === 'old') localStorage.setItem('look', m[1]); else localStorage.removeItem('look'); }
        lp = localStorage.getItem('look');
    } catch (e) { var q = /[?&]look=(plain|old)(&|$)/.exec(location.search); lp = q ? q[1] : null; }
    if (lp === 'plain' || (lp !== 'old' && true)) {
        document.documentElement.classList.add('look-plain');
    }
})();
</script>

        function getCookie(name) {
            const nameEQ = name + '=';
            const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
            for (let i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) { let c = ca[i].trim(); if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length); }
            return null;
        }

        function gtag(){dataLayer.push(arguments);}
        function loadGA() {
            if (window.__gaLoaded) return;
            window.__gaLoaded = true;

            s.async = true; s.src = 'https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=G-ZXDJNBML2H';

            gtag('js', new Date()); gtag('config', 'G-ZXDJNBML2H');
        }
        if (getCookie('cookie_consent') === 'accepted') {
            const boot = () => ('requestIdleCallback' in window) ? requestIdleCallback(loadGA, { timeout: 4000 }) : setTimeout(loadGA, 1000);

// analytics.js is deferred, so page scripts that run while the page is still
// loading (paywall "shown" events, hub page views) would find no ssTrack and be
// dropped. Queue them here; analytics.js replays the queue when it starts.
window.ssTrack = window.ssTrack || function () {
    if (window.__ssTrackReal) return window.__ssTrackReal.apply(null, arguments);
    (window.__ssq = window.__ssq || []).push([].slice.call(arguments));
};
// A/B split for the funnel tests: 'a' (control), 'b', or null when the test is
// off (AB_TESTS env). Keyed on the analytics device id, so a visitor keeps the
// same side across pages and cached HTML. Logs ab_exposure once per test/page.
window.SS_AB_ON = ["monthly_first_market"];
window.ssAB = window.ssAB || function (test) {
    try {
        if ((window.SS_AB_ON || []).indexOf(test) === -1) return null;
        var k = 'ss_visitor_id', id = localStorage.getItem(k);
        if (!id) {
            id = 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(/[xy]/g, function (c) {
                var r = Math.random() * 16 | 0; return (c === 'x' ? r : (r & 3 | 8)).toString(16); });
            localStorage.setItem(k, id);
        }
        // FNV-1a, then a high bit: a plain *31 hash's low bit only counts odd
        // character codes, which ties every test's split to every other's.
        var s = test + ':' + id, h = 2166136261;
        for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) { h ^= s.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619); }
        var v = ((h >>> 16) & 1) ? 'b' : 'a';
        var seen = window.__ssABSeen = window.__ssABSeen || {};
        if (!seen[test]) { seen[test] = 1; window.ssTrack('ab_exposure', { test: test, variant: v }); }
        return v;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
};

(function(){
  if (window.__ssMetaPixel) return; window.__ssMetaPixel = true;
  if (!window.fbq) {

  }
  function loadPixel(){
    if (window.__ssMetaPixelLoaded) return; window.__ssMetaPixelLoaded = true;
    var s = document.createElement('script');
    s.async = true;
    s.src = 'https://connect.facebook.net/en_US/fbevents.js';

    // cleared client-side so edge-cached HTML never bakes the event in.

  // case the banner script runs after this include.

      wrapped.__ssPixelWrapped = true;
      window.handleConsent = wrapped;
    }
  }
  patch();
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', patch);
})();

    /* ================================================================
       v2: the plain look (design_briefs/15_QUICK_SEARCH.md). On when the

    [data-theme="dark"] .pp-drawer.pp-v2.pp-v2.pp-v2, [data-theme="dark"] .pp-scrim.pp-v2.pp-v2.pp-v2,
    html.theme-v2:not([data-theme="light"]) .pp-drawer.pp-v2.pp-v2.pp-v2, html.theme-v2:not([data-theme="light"]) .pp-scrim.pp-v2.pp-v2.pp-v2 {

<div class="pp-scrim" id="ppScrim"></div>
<aside class="pp-drawer" id="ppDrawer" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="ppTitle">
    <div class="pp-header">
        <h2 class="pp-title" id="ppTitle">Search players</h2>
        <button class="pp-close" id="ppClose" aria-label="Close">
            <i class="fas fa-times"></i>
        </button>
    </div>
    <div class="pp-searchbar">
        <i class="fas fa-search pp-search-icon"></i>
        <input type="text" class="pp-search" id="ppSearch"
               placeholder="Search 89,000+ players by name…" autocomplete="off"
               role="combobox" aria-expanded="true" aria-autocomplete="list">
    </div>
    <div class="pp-tabs" role="tablist">

<script>
(function() {
    // Guard: only initialise once even if the partial is accidentally included twice.
    if (window.__playerPickerLoaded) return;
    window.__playerPickerLoaded = true;

    const RECENT_KEY = 'pp_recent_players';
    const RECENT_MAX = 12;
    const HOME_RECENT_ROWS = 5;      // collapsed Recent section size
    const HOME_LEAGUE_ROWS = 8;      // "Browse by league" shortcut count
    const CLUB_RESULT_ROWS = 3;      // Clubs shown above player results
    const DEFAULT_SELECT = (player) => {
        const id = player.value || player.player_id;
        if (id) window.location.href = '/player/' + id + '/';
    };

    // Tier flags from Jinja context. Used to short-circuit locked panes so we don't
    // hit APIs we know will 403.
    const HAS_WATCHLIST_ACCESS = false;

    // v2 is the default (see the header comment): ?search=1 brings the earlier pane back in
    // this browser and is remembered, ?search=2 clears that.
    let V2 = true;
    try {
        const m = /[?&]search=(\d)(&|$)/.exec(location.search);
        if (m) { if (m[1] === '1') localStorage.setItem('pp_v', '1'); else localStorage.removeItem('pp_v'); }
        V2 = localStorage.getItem('pp_v') !== '1';
    } catch (e) {}
    const PLACE_ROWS = 3;            // clubs, and leagues, shown above the players (v2)
    const LOCALE_PRE = /^\/es(\/|$)/.test(location.pathname) ? '/es' : '';
    const T2 = {
        search:   "Search",
        phAll:    "Search players, clubs and leagues",
        clubs:    "Clubs",
        leagues:  "Leagues",
        players:  "Players",
        hint:     "Try a player, a club or a league.",
        none:     "Nothing matches that.",
    };
    const IS_LOGGED_IN = false;
    const PP_ANALYST = false;
    const PP_PRICE = "Scout \u00b7 \u00a314.92 a month billed yearly, or \u00a319.99 monthly";
    const PP_UPGRADE = '/premium/scouting?plan=scout&source=player-picker-drawer';

    const state = {
        open: false,
        pane: 'home',
        onSelect: DEFAULT_SELECT,
        query: '',
        searchDebounce: null,
        lastResults: [],
        lastClubs: [],
        lastLeagues: [],
        go: false,                     // v2: the sitewide search, where clubs and leagues open their pages
        recentExpanded: false,
        cursor: -1,                    // keyboard-selected row index in the active pane
        // Leagues view: stack starts at the flat directory (no continent drilling).
        browseStack: [{ level: 'directory', id: null, label: 'Leagues' }],
        browseCache: {},
        directoryFilter: '',
        // Watchlist state: null = list view; set to {id, name} when viewing a
        // specific watchlist's players. Cache key is watchlist id.
        watchlistView: null,
        watchlistsCache: null,
        watchlistPlayersCache: {},
        returnFocus: null,             // element to refocus on close (opts.returnFocus)
    };

    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    const TITLE0 = $('ppTitle').textContent, PH0 = $('ppSearch').placeholder;

    function escapeHtml(s) {
        return String(s == null ? '' : s)
            .replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;')
            .replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
    }

    // Upgrade CTA card — same palette as the rest of the drawer, no decorations.
    function upgradeCardHtml(ctx) {
        const title = escapeHtml(ctx.title || 'Scout feature');
        const body  = escapeHtml(ctx.body  || '');
        const cta   = escapeHtml(PP_ANALYST ? 'Add scouting' : (ctx.cta || 'Open every league'));
        const loginLine = IS_LOGGED_IN ? ''
            : '<div class="pp-upgrade-muted">Already a member? <a href="/login">Sign in</a></div>';
        return '<div class="pp-upgrade-card">' +
            '<h4>' + title + '</h4>' +
            (body ? '<p>' + body + '</p>' : '') +
            '<a class="pp-upgrade-btn" href="' + PP_UPGRADE + '" data-copy="v3">' + cta + '</a>' +
            '<div class="pp-upgrade-muted">' + escapeHtml(PP_PRICE) + '</div>' +
            loginLine +
            '</div>';
    }

    // ----- open / close / pane switching -----
    function open(opts) {
        opts = opts || {};
        state.open = true;
        state.onSelect = typeof opts.onSelect === 'function' ? opts.onSelect : DEFAULT_SELECT;
        if (V2) {
            // The sitewide search (the navbar says so, or nobody is waiting for a player back):
            // its own title and prompt, set on every open because the drawer is shared.
            state.go = opts.entities === true || typeof opts.onSelect !== 'function';
            $('ppTitle').textContent = state.go ? T2.search : (opts.title || TITLE0);
            $('ppSearch').placeholder = state.go ? T2.phAll : PH0;
        } else if (opts.title) $('ppTitle').textContent = opts.title;
        state.returnFocus = opts.returnFocus || null;
        if (opts.instant) {
            $('ppScrim').classList.add('pp-instant');
            $('ppDrawer').classList.add('pp-instant');
        }
        $('ppScrim').classList.add('open');
        $('ppDrawer').classList.add('open');
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        // Legacy pane names: 'search' and 'recent' both live in the Home view now.
        let pane = opts.defaultPane || 'home';
        if (pane === 'search' || pane === 'recent') pane = 'home';
        switchPane(pane);
        if (opts.instant) $('ppSearch').focus();
        else setTimeout(() => $('ppSearch').focus(), 280);
    }

    function close() {
        state.open = false;
        $('ppScrim').classList.remove('pp-instant');
        $('ppDrawer').classList.remove('pp-instant');
        $('ppScrim').classList.remove('open');
        $('ppDrawer').classList.remove('open');
        document.body.style.overflow = '';
        $('ppSearch').value = '';
        state.query = '';
        state.cursor = -1;
        state.recentExpanded = false;
        state.watchlistView = null;
        const rf = state.returnFocus;
        state.returnFocus = null;
        if (rf && typeof rf.focus === 'function') {
            try { rf.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) {}
        }
    }

    function switchPane(pane) {
        state.pane = pane;
        state.cursor = -1;
        document.querySelectorAll('.pp-tab').forEach(t =>
            t.classList.toggle('active', t.dataset.pane === pane));
        document.querySelectorAll('.pp-pane').forEach(p =>
            p.classList.toggle('active', p.dataset.pane === pane));
        if (pane === 'home') renderHome();
        if (pane === 'browse') renderBrowse();
        if (pane === 'watchlists') renderWatchlists();
    }

    // Commit a selection — persist to recent, fire onSelect, close drawer.
    function commit(player) {
        persistRecent(player);
        close();
        try { state.onSelect(player); } catch (e) { console.error('PlayerPicker onSelect error:', e); }
    }

    // ----- Shared row builders -----
    function playerRowHtml(p, extraCls) {
        const name = escapeHtml(p.display_name || p.label || p.name || '');
        const meta = escapeHtml(p.meta || p.team || '');
        const img  = escapeHtml(p.image_path || '/static/images/default-player.png');
        const tag  = p.tag ? '<span class="pp-row-tag">' + escapeHtml(String(p.tag)) + '</span>' : '';
        const path = p.path ? '" data-path="' + escapeHtml(p.path) : '';
        return '<div class="pp-row' + (extraCls ? ' ' + extraCls : '') + '" data-pid="' + p.value +
            '" data-name="' + name + '" data-team="' + escapeHtml(p.team || '') +
            '" data-img="' + img + path + '">' +
            '<img class="pp-row-img" src="' + img + '" loading="lazy"' +
            ' onerror="this.src=\'/static/images/default-player.png\'">' +
            '<div class="pp-row-main">' +
                '<div class="pp-row-name">' + name + '</div>' +
                (meta ? '<div class="pp-row-meta">' + meta + '</div>' : '') +
            '</div>' + tag + '</div>';
    }

    function bindPlayerRows(container) {
        container.querySelectorAll('.pp-row[data-pid]').forEach(row => {
            if (row.classList.contains('locked')) {
                row.style.opacity = '';
                row.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    window.location.href = '/login?next=' + encodeURIComponent('/player/' + row.dataset.pid + '/');
                });
                return;
            }
            row.addEventListener('click', () => commit({
                value: parseInt(row.dataset.pid, 10),
                display_name: row.dataset.name,
                team: row.dataset.team,
                image_path: row.dataset.img,
                path: row.dataset.path || null,
            }));
        });
    }

    // Club row — visually a league row (crest + name + meta), but it opens the
    // squad rather than a club grid.
    function clubRowHtml(c) {
        const label = escapeHtml(c.label || '');
        const crest = c.image_path
            ? '<img class="pp-row-crest" src="' + escapeHtml(c.image_path) + '" loading="lazy"' +
              ' onerror="this.outerHTML=\'<span class=\\\'pp-row-icon\\\'><i class=\\\'fas fa-shield-alt\\\'></i></span>\'">'
            : '<span class="pp-row-icon"><i class="fas fa-shield-alt"></i></span>';
        const bits = [];
        if (c.league) bits.push(escapeHtml(c.league));
        if (c.player_count) bits.push(c.player_count + (c.player_count === 1 ? ' player' : ' players'));
        return '<div class="pp-row" data-club-id="' + c.id + '" data-club-label="' + label + '">' +
            crest +
            '<div class="pp-row-main">' +
                '<div class="pp-row-name">' + label + '</div>' +
                (bits.length ? '<div class="pp-row-meta">' + bits.join(' · ') + '</div>' : '') +
            '</div></div>';
    }

    function bindClubRows(container) {
        container.querySelectorAll('.pp-row[data-club-id]').forEach(row => {
            row.addEventListener('click', () => {
                // Drop straight to the squad; the breadcrumb's "Leagues" root is
                // still there to climb back out.
                state.browseStack = [
                    { level: 'directory', id: null, label: 'Leagues' },
                    { level: 'squad', id: row.dataset.clubId, label: row.dataset.clubLabel },
                ];
                switchPane('browse');
            });
        });
    }

    // v2, sitewide search: a club or a league is a link to its own page (so it can be
    // opened in a new tab), with the crest, the name and one line of where it is.
    function placeRowHtml(kind, it) {
        const label = escapeHtml(it.label || '');
        const meta  = escapeHtml(kind === 'club' ? (it.league || '') : (it.country || ''));
        const crest = it.image_path
            ? '<img class="pp-row-crest" src="' + escapeHtml(it.image_path) + '" alt="" loading="lazy"' +
              ' onerror="this.style.visibility=\'hidden\'">'
            : '<span class="pp-row-icon"></span>';
        return '<a class="pp-row" href="' + escapeHtml(LOCALE_PRE + (it.href || '#')) + '" data-place="' + kind +
            '" data-place-id="' + escapeHtml(String(it.id)) + '" data-name="' + label + '">' + crest +
            '<div class="pp-row-main"><div class="pp-row-name">' + label + '</div>' +
            (meta ? '<div class="pp-row-meta">' + meta + '</div>' : '') +
            '</div><span class="pp-row-go" aria-hidden="true">→</span></a>';
    }

    function bindPlaceRows(container) {
        container.querySelectorAll('a.pp-row[data-place]').forEach(row => {
            row.addEventListener('click', () => {
                try {
                    if (typeof window.ssTrack === 'function') window.ssTrack('search_select', {
                        feature: 'navbar_search', kind: row.dataset.place, id: row.dataset.placeId, name: row.dataset.name,
                    });
                } catch (e) {}
            });
        });
    }

    function secHtml(label, actionId, actionLabel) {
        return '<div class="pp-sec"><span class="pp-sec-label">' + escapeHtml(label) + '</span>' +
            (actionId ? '<button class="pp-sec-action" id="' + actionId + '">' + escapeHtml(actionLabel) + '</button>' : '') +
            '</div>';
    }

    // ----- Home view -----
    function renderHome() {
        if (state.query.length >= 2) { renderResults(state.lastResults, false); return; }
        renderHomeEmpty();
    }

    function renderHomeEmpty() {
        const container = $('ppHomeContent');
        let html = '';

        // 1. Recent
        const recent = readRecent();
        if (recent.length) {
            const shown = state.recentExpanded ? recent : recent.slice(0, HOME_RECENT_ROWS);
            const needsToggle = recent.length > HOME_RECENT_ROWS;
            html += secHtml('Recent', 'ppRecentAction',
                state.recentExpanded ? 'Show fewer' : (needsToggle ? 'Show all (' + recent.length + ')' : 'Clear'));
            html += '<div class="pp-list">' + shown.map(p => playerRowHtml(p)).join('') + '</div>';
            if (state.recentExpanded || !needsToggle) {
                // When fully expanded (or no toggle needed) the action slot is Clear —
                // rendered above when !needsToggle; add a separate clear when expanded.
                if (state.recentExpanded) {
                    html += '<div style="text-align:right;padding:2px 8px;">' +
                        '<button class="pp-sec-action" id="ppRecentClear">Clear recent</button></div>';
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Watchlists shortcut (Scout+ with lists) — filled in async below.
        if (HAS_WATCHLIST_ACCESS) html += '<div id="ppHomeWatchlists"></div>';

        // 3. Browse by league — filled in async from the directory payload.
        html += '<div id="ppHomeLeagues"></div>';

        if (!recent.length) {
            html += state.go ? '<div class="pp-hint">' + escapeHtml(T2.hint) + '</div>'
                : '<div class="pp-hint">Try a name — 89,000+ players across world football.</div>';
        }

        container.innerHTML = html;
        bindPlayerRows(container);
        state.cursor = -1;

        const action = $('ppRecentAction');
        if (action) action.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (recent.length > HOME_RECENT_ROWS) {
                state.recentExpanded = !state.recentExpanded;
                renderHomeEmpty();
            } else {
                clearRecent();
            }
        });
        const clearBtn = $('ppRecentClear');
        if (clearBtn) clearBtn.addEventListener('click', clearRecent);

        renderHomeWatchlists();
        renderHomeLeagues();
    }

    function renderHomeWatchlists() {
        const slot = $('ppHomeWatchlists');
        if (!slot || !HAS_WATCHLIST_ACCESS) return;
        const paint = (lists) => {
            if (!lists || !lists.length) { slot.innerHTML = ''; return; }
            slot.innerHTML = secHtml('Your watchlists', 'ppHomeWlAll', 'View all') +
                '<div class="pp-list">' + lists.slice(0, 3).map(wl =>
                    '<div class="pp-row" data-wlid="' + wl.id + '" data-wlname="' + escapeHtml(wl.name) + '">' +
                        '<span class="pp-row-icon"><i class="far fa-bookmark"></i></span>' +
                        '<div class="pp-row-main">' +
                            '<div class="pp-row-name">' + escapeHtml(wl.name) + '</div>' +
                            '<div class="pp-row-meta">' + (wl.player_count || 0) + ' ' +
                                ((wl.player_count || 0) === 1 ? 'player' : 'players') + '</div>' +
                        '</div></div>'
                ).join('') + '</div>';
            slot.querySelectorAll('.pp-row[data-wlid]').forEach(row => {
                row.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    state.watchlistView = { id: parseInt(row.dataset.wlid, 10), name: row.dataset.wlname };
                    switchPane('watchlists');
                });
            });
            const all = $('ppHomeWlAll');
            if (all) all.addEventListener('click', () => { state.watchlistView = null; switchPane('watchlists'); });
        };
        if (state.watchlistsCache) { paint(state.watchlistsCache); return; }
        fetch('/api/watchlists')
            .then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : { watchlists: [] })
            .then(body => {
                state.watchlistsCache = (body && body.watchlists) || [];
                paint(state.watchlistsCache);
            })
            .catch(() => { slot.innerHTML = ''; });
    }

    function renderHomeLeagues() {
        const slot = $('ppHomeLeagues');
        if (!slot) return;
        const paint = (payload) => {
            const accessible = (payload.items || []).filter(it => !it.locked).slice(0, HOME_LEAGUE_ROWS);
            if (!accessible.length) { slot.innerHTML = ''; return; }
            slot.innerHTML = secHtml('Browse by league', 'ppHomeLgAll', 'All leagues') +
                '<div class="pp-list">' + accessible.map(leagueRowHtml).join('') + '</div>';
            bindLeagueRows(slot);
            const all = $('ppHomeLgAll');
            if (all) all.addEventListener('click', () => {
                state.browseStack = [{ level: 'directory', id: null, label: 'Leagues' }];
                switchPane('browse');
            });
        };
        if (state.browseCache['directory:']) { paint(state.browseCache['directory:']); return; }
        fetch('/api/scout-hub/browse?level=directory')
            .then(r => r.json())
            .then(body => {
                if (!body || body.error) { slot.innerHTML = ''; return; }
                state.browseCache['directory:'] = body;
                paint(body);
            })
            .catch(() => { slot.innerHTML = ''; });
    }

    // ----- Search results -----
    function wireSearch() {
        const input = $('ppSearch');
        input.addEventListener('input', function() {
            clearTimeout(state.searchDebounce);
            state.query = this.value.trim();
            state.cursor = -1;
            if (state.pane !== 'home') switchPane('home');
            if (state.query.length < 2) {
                state.lastResults = []; state.lastClubs = []; state.lastLeagues = [];
                renderHomeEmpty(); return;
            }
            const q = state.query;
            state.searchDebounce = setTimeout(() => {
                renderResults(null, true);
                // Players and clubs are separate endpoints; wait for both so the
                // list paints once rather than reflowing under the cursor.
                Promise.all([
                    fetch('/autocomplete?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q))
                        .then(r => r.json()).catch(() => null),
                    // v2: clubs and leagues in one request; before it, clubs only
                    V2 ? fetch('/api/search/places?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q))
                            .then(r => r.json()).then(b => ({ clubs: (b && b.clubs) || [], leagues: (b && b.leagues) || [] }))
                            .catch(() => ({ clubs: [], leagues: [] }))
                       : fetch('/api/scout-hub/club-search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q))
                            .then(r => r.json()).then(b => ({ clubs: (b && b.items) || [], leagues: [] }))
                            .catch(() => ({ clubs: [], leagues: [] })),
                ]).then(([players, places]) => {
                    if (q !== state.query) return;  // stale response
                    if (players === null) {
                        $('ppHomeContent').innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">Search failed — try again.</div>';
                        return;
                    }
                    state.lastResults = players || [];
                    state.lastClubs = places.clubs;
                    state.lastLeagues = places.leagues;
                    renderResults(state.lastResults, false);
                });
            }, 250);
        });

        // Keyboard navigation — input keeps focus; arrows move the row cursor.
        input.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (e.key !== 'ArrowDown' && e.key !== 'ArrowUp' && e.key !== 'Enter') return;
            const pane = document.querySelector('.pp-pane.active');
            const rows = pane ? Array.from(pane.querySelectorAll('.pp-row:not(.locked)')) : [];
            if (!rows.length) return;
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                e.preventDefault();
                const target = rows[state.cursor] || rows[0];
                if (target) target.click();
                return;
            }
            e.preventDefault();
            const delta = e.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : -1;
            state.cursor = Math.max(0, Math.min(rows.length - 1, state.cursor + delta));
            rows.forEach((r, i) => r.classList.toggle('kb', i === state.cursor));
            rows[state.cursor].scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
        });
    }

    function renderResults(players, loading) {
        const container = $('ppHomeContent');
        if (loading) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-loading"><i class="fas fa-circle-notch"></i> Searching…</div>';
            return;
        }
        if (V2) { renderResultsV2(players); return; }
        const clubs = (state.lastClubs || []).slice(0, CLUB_RESULT_ROWS);
        let html = '';

        // Clubs lead — a club name rarely collides with a player name, so when
        // one matches it's almost always what was meant ("man utd", "villa").
        if (clubs.length) {
            html += secHtml('Clubs') +
                '<div class="pp-list">' + clubs.map(clubRowHtml).join('') + '</div>';
        }

        if (!players || !players.length) {
            html += clubs.length
                ? ''
                : '<div class="pp-empty">Nothing matches that name.</div>';
        } else {
            html += (clubs.length ? secHtml('Players') : '') +
                '<div class="pp-count">' + players.length + (players.length === 1 ? ' result' : ' results') + '</div>' +
                '<div class="pp-list">' + players.map(p => {
                    const bits = [];
                    if (p.position) bits.push(p.position);
                    if (p.age) bits.push(p.age + ' yrs');
                    if (p.team) bits.push(p.team);
                    return playerRowHtml({
                        value: p.value,
                        display_name: p.display_name || p.label || p.name,
                        team: p.team || '',
                        image_path: p.image_path,
                        path: p.path || null,
                        meta: bits.join(' · '),
                        tag: p.locked ? 'Free' : '',
                    }, p.locked ? 'locked' : '');
                }).join('') + '</div>';
        }

        // Short queries: keep recents visible (dimmed) under the results.
        const recent = readRecent();
        if (state.query.length < 4 && recent.length) {
            html += '<div class="pp-divider"></div>' + secHtml('Recent') +
                '<div class="pp-list pp-dim">' +
                recent.slice(0, 3).map(p => playerRowHtml(p)).join('') + '</div>';
        }

        container.innerHTML = html;
        bindPlayerRows(container);
        bindClubRows(container);
        state.cursor = -1;
    }

    // v2 results: Clubs, Leagues, Players, in that order. A club or league name rarely
    // collides with a player's, so when one matches it is almost always what was meant and
    // Enter takes it. In the sitewide search they open their own pages; where a page is
    // waiting for a player they drill down to a squad, as before.
    function playerResultHtml(p) {
        const bits = [];
        if (p.position) bits.push(p.position);
        if (p.age) bits.push(p.age + ' yrs');
        if (p.team) bits.push(p.team);
        return playerRowHtml({
            value: p.value,
            display_name: p.display_name || p.label || p.name,
            team: p.team || '',
            image_path: p.image_path,
            path: p.path || null,
            meta: bits.join(' · '),
            tag: p.locked ? 'Free' : '',
        }, p.locked ? 'locked' : '');
    }

    function renderResultsV2(players) {
        const container = $('ppHomeContent');
        const clubs = (state.lastClubs || []).slice(0, PLACE_ROWS);
        const leagues = (state.lastLeagues || []).slice(0, PLACE_ROWS);
        const places = clubs.length + leagues.length;
        // A strong match (the name, the start of the name, the short code) leads the list, so
        // Enter takes it. A weak one ("sak" inside Bnei Sakhnin, a league found by its country)
        // goes under the players, so it never stands in front of the player who was meant.
        const strong = (list) => list.length && (list[0].rank == null || list[0].rank <= 1);
        const clubsHtml = !clubs.length ? '' : secHtml(T2.clubs) + '<div class="pp-list">' +
            clubs.map(c => state.go ? placeRowHtml('club', c) : clubRowHtml(c)).join('') + '</div>';
        const leaguesHtml = !leagues.length ? '' : secHtml(T2.leagues) + '<div class="pp-list">' +
            leagues.map(l => state.go ? placeRowHtml('league', l) : leagueRowHtml(l)).join('') + '</div>';
        const hasPlayers = !!(players && players.length);
        const playersHtml = !hasPlayers ? '' :
            (places ? secHtml(T2.players) : '<div class="pp-count">' + players.length +
                (players.length === 1 ? ' result' : ' results') + '</div>') +
            '<div class="pp-list">' + players.map(playerResultHtml).join('') + '</div>';
        let html = (strong(clubs) || !hasPlayers ? clubsHtml : '') + (strong(leagues) || !hasPlayers ? leaguesHtml : '') + playersHtml +
            (hasPlayers && !strong(clubs) ? clubsHtml : '') + (hasPlayers && !strong(leagues) ? leaguesHtml : '');
        if (!places && !hasPlayers) html = '<div class="pp-empty">' + escapeHtml(T2.none) + '</div>';
        container.innerHTML = html;
        bindPlayerRows(container);
        if (state.go) bindPlaceRows(container);
        else { bindClubRows(container); bindLeagueRows(container); }
        state.cursor = -1;
    }

    // ----- Leagues view (flat directory → clubs → squad) -----
    function leagueRowHtml(it) {
        const label  = escapeHtml(it.label);
        const locked = !!it.locked;
        const crest  = it.image_path
            ? '<img class="pp-row-crest" src="' + escapeHtml(it.image_path) + '" loading="lazy"' +
              ' onerror="this.outerHTML=\'<span class=\\\'pp-row-icon\\\'><i class=\\\'fas fa-trophy\\\'></i></span>\'">'
            : '<span class="pp-row-icon"><i class="fas fa-trophy"></i></span>';
        return '<div class="pp-row' + (locked ? ' locked' : '') + '" data-league-id="' + it.id +
            '" data-label="' + label + '"' + (locked ? ' data-locked="1"' : '') + '>' +
            crest +
            '<div class="pp-row-main">' +
                '<div class="pp-row-name">' + label + '</div>' +
                (it.country ? '<div class="pp-row-meta">' + escapeHtml(it.country) + '</div>' : '') +
            '</div>' +
            (locked ? '<span class="pp-row-lock"><i class="fas fa-lock"></i></span>' : '') +
            '</div>';
    }

    function bindLeagueRows(container) {
        container.querySelectorAll('.pp-row[data-league-id]').forEach(row => {
            row.addEventListener('click', () => {
                if (row.dataset.locked === '1') {
                    // Jump to the Leagues view and pitch the upgrade there.
                    if (state.pane !== 'browse') switchPane('browse');
                    renderBrowseUpgradeCard(row.dataset.label);
                    return;
                }
                state.browseStack = [
                    { level: 'directory', id: null, label: 'Leagues' },
                    { level: 'club', id: row.dataset.leagueId, label: row.dataset.label },
                ];
                if (state.pane !== 'browse') switchPane('browse'); else renderBrowse();
            });
        });
    }

    function renderBrowse() {
        renderBreadcrumb();
        const node = state.browseStack[state.browseStack.length - 1];
        const container = $('ppBrowseContent');
        const cacheKey = node.level + ':' + (node.id == null ? '' : node.id);
        if (state.browseCache[cacheKey]) {
            paintBrowse(container, state.browseCache[cacheKey], node.level);
            return;
        }
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-loading"><i class="fas fa-circle-notch"></i> Loading…</div>';
        const params = new URLSearchParams({ level: node.level });
        if (node.id != null) params.set('parent_id', node.id);
        fetch('/api/scout-hub/browse?' + params.toString())
            .then(async r => ({ status: r.status, body: await r.json().catch(() => ({})) }))
            .then(({ status, body }) => {
                if (body && body.upgrade) {
                    renderBrowseUpgradeCard(node.label);
                    return;
                }
                if (body && body.error) {
                    container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">' + escapeHtml(body.error) + '</div>';
                    return;
                }
                state.browseCache[cacheKey] = body;
                paintBrowse(container, body, node.level);
            })
            .catch(err => {
                console.error('PlayerPicker browse error:', err);
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">Couldn\'t load — try again.</div>';
            });
    }

    function paintBrowse(container, data, currentLevel) {
        if (currentLevel === 'directory') {
            container.innerHTML = renderDirectory(data.items);
            const filter = $('ppDirFilter');
            if (filter) {
                filter.value = state.directoryFilter;
                filter.addEventListener('input', function() {
                    state.directoryFilter = this.value.trim().toLowerCase();
                    $('ppDirGroups').innerHTML = renderDirectoryGroups(data.items);
                    bindLeagueRows($('ppDirGroups'));
                });
            }
            bindLeagueRows(container);
            return;
        }
        if (data.level === 'squad') {
            container.innerHTML = renderSquadList(data.items);
            bindPlayerRows(container);
            return;
        }
        // Clubs — grid of crest cards.
        container.innerHTML = renderClubGrid(data.items);
        container.querySelectorAll('.pp-browse-card').forEach(card => {
            card.addEventListener('click', () => {
                state.browseStack.push({
                    level: 'squad',
                    id:    card.dataset.id,
                    label: card.dataset.label,
                });
                renderBrowse();
            });
        });
    }

    function renderDirectory(items) {
        if (!items || !items.length) return '<div class="pp-empty">Nothing to show here.</div>';
        return '<div class="pp-filter-wrap">' +
            '<i class="fas fa-search pp-filter-icon"></i>' +
            '<input type="text" class="pp-filter" id="ppDirFilter" placeholder="Filter leagues or countries…">' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div id="ppDirGroups">' + renderDirectoryGroups(items) + '</div>';
    }

    function renderDirectoryGroups(items) {
        let list = items;
        if (state.directoryFilter) {
            const f = state.directoryFilter;
            list = items.filter(it =>
                (it.label || '').toLowerCase().includes(f) ||
                (it.country || '').toLowerCase().includes(f));
        }
        if (!list.length) return '<div class="pp-empty">No leagues match that filter.</div>';

        // Group by region; regions ordered by accessible-league count (desc) so the
        // user's unlocked content leads. Within a region: accessible first (server
        // order = category/tier), locked after, muted.
        const groups = {};
        list.forEach(it => {
            const key = it.region || 'World';
            (groups[key] = groups[key] || []).push(it);
        });
        const regionOrder = Object.keys(groups).sort((a, b) => {
            const acc = (g) => groups[g].filter(it => !it.locked).length;
            return acc(b) - acc(a) || a.localeCompare(b);
        });
        return regionOrder.map(region => {
            const rows = groups[region];
            const accessible = rows.filter(it => !it.locked);
            const locked = rows.filter(it => it.locked);
            return '<div class="pp-region">' + escapeHtml(region) + '</div>' +
                '<div class="pp-list">' +
                accessible.map(leagueRowHtml).join('') +
                locked.map(leagueRowHtml).join('') +
                '</div>';
        }).join('');
    }

    function renderClubGrid(items) {
        if (!items || !items.length) return '<div class="pp-empty">Nothing to show here.</div>';
        return '<div class="pp-browse-grid">' + items.map(it => {
            const label = escapeHtml(it.label);
            const meta  = escapeHtml(it.meta || '');
            const visual = it.image_path
                ? '<img class="pp-browse-card-img" src="' + escapeHtml(it.image_path) + '" loading="lazy"' +
                  ' onerror="this.outerHTML=\'<div class=\\\'pp-browse-card-img\\\'><i class=\\\'fas fa-shield-alt\\\'></i></div>\'">'
                : '<div class="pp-browse-card-img"><i class="fas fa-shield-alt"></i></div>';
            return '<div class="pp-browse-card" data-id="' + escapeHtml(String(it.id)) +
                '" data-label="' + label + '">' +
                visual +
                '<div class="pp-browse-card-name">' + label + '</div>' +
                '<div class="pp-browse-card-meta">' + meta + '</div>' +
                '</div>';
        }).join('') + '</div>';
    }

    function renderSquadList(items) {
        if (!items || !items.length) return '<div class="pp-empty">No players in this squad.</div>';
        return '<div class="pp-list">' + items.map(p => playerRowHtml({
            value: p.value,
            display_name: p.label,
            team: p.league || '',
            meta: p.league || '',
            image_path: p.image_path,
            tag: p.rating || null,
        })).join('') + '</div>';
    }

    // Swap the browse content for an upgrade pitch; user can tap the breadcrumb to
    // go back to the directory without losing their place.
    function renderBrowseUpgradeCard(entityLabel) {
        const safeLabel = escapeHtml(entityLabel || 'This league');
        $('ppBrowseContent').innerHTML = upgradeCardHtml({
            title: 'Every player in ' + safeLabel,
            body: 'Every league we track, player by player.',
            cta: 'Open every league',
        });
    }

    function renderBreadcrumb() {
        const wrap = $('ppBreadcrumb');
        wrap.innerHTML = state.browseStack.map((n, i) => {
            const last = i === state.browseStack.length - 1;
            return '<button class="' + (last ? 'current' : '') + '" data-idx="' + i + '">' +
                escapeHtml(n.label) + '</button>' +
                (last ? '' : '<span class="pp-breadcrumb-sep">›</span>');
        }).join('');
        wrap.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                state.browseStack = state.browseStack.slice(0, parseInt(btn.dataset.idx, 10) + 1);
                renderBrowse();
            });
        });
    }

    // ----- Recent storage -----
    function readRecent() {
        try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(RECENT_KEY) || '[]'); }
        catch (e) { return []; }
    }
    function persistRecent(player) {
        if (!player || !player.value) return;
        try {
            const list = readRecent().filter(p => p.value !== player.value);
            list.unshift({
                value: player.value,
                display_name: player.display_name || player.label || '',
                team: player.team || '',
                image_path: player.image_path || '',
                path: player.path || null,
                ts: Date.now(),
            });
            localStorage.setItem(RECENT_KEY, JSON.stringify(list.slice(0, RECENT_MAX)));
        } catch (e) {}
    }
    function clearRecent() {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(RECENT_KEY); } catch (e) {}
        state.recentExpanded = false;
        renderHomeEmpty();
    }

    // ----- Watchlists view -----
    function renderWatchlists() {
        const container = $('ppWatchlistsContent');
        if (!container) return;
        if (state.watchlistView) {
            renderWatchlistPlayers(state.watchlistView);
        } else {
            renderWatchlistsList();
        }
    }

    function renderWatchlistsList() {
        const container = $('ppWatchlistsContent');
        $('ppWatchlistsCrumb').style.display = 'none';

        // Non-Scout tiers see an upgrade CTA instead of probing the API. Anon users
        // get a sign-in nudge with the same layout.
        if (!IS_LOGGED_IN) {
            container.innerHTML = upgradeCardHtml({
                title: 'Pick players from your watchlists',
                body: 'Save players into curated lists and pick them instantly from any page.',
                cta: 'Keep watchlists',
            });
            return;
        }
        if (!HAS_WATCHLIST_ACCESS) {
            container.innerHTML = upgradeCardHtml({
                title: 'Pick players from your watchlists',
                body: 'Save players into curated lists and pick them instantly from any page.',
                cta: 'Keep watchlists',
            });
            return;
        }

        const paint = (watchlists) => {
            if (!watchlists.length) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">' +
                    'You don\'t have any watchlists yet. ' +
                    '<a href="/scout-hub/watchlists">Create one</a> to save players for quick access.' +
                    '</div>';
                return;
            }
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-list" style="margin-top:10px;">' + watchlists.map(wl => {
                const name = escapeHtml(wl.name);
                const count = wl.player_count || 0;
                return '<div class="pp-row" data-wlid="' + wl.id + '" data-wlname="' + name + '">' +
                    '<span class="pp-row-icon"><i class="far fa-bookmark"></i></span>' +
                    '<div class="pp-row-main">' +
                        '<div class="pp-row-name">' + name + '</div>' +
                        '<div class="pp-row-meta">' + count + ' ' + (count === 1 ? 'player' : 'players') + '</div>' +
                    '</div></div>';
            }).join('') + '</div>';
            container.querySelectorAll('.pp-row[data-wlid]').forEach(row => {
                row.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    state.watchlistView = {
                        id: parseInt(row.dataset.wlid, 10),
                        name: row.dataset.wlname,
                    };
                    renderWatchlistPlayers(state.watchlistView);
                });
            });
        };

        if (state.watchlistsCache) { paint(state.watchlistsCache); return; }

        container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-loading"><i class="fas fa-circle-notch"></i> Loading…</div>';
        fetch('/api/watchlists')
            .then(async r => ({ status: r.status, body: await r.json().catch(() => ({})) }))
            .then(({ status, body }) => {
                if (status === 403) {
                    container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">' +
                        'Watchlists are a Scout feature. ' +
                        '<a href="' + PP_UPGRADE + '">' + (PP_ANALYST ? 'Add scouting' : 'Keep watchlists') + '</a>.' +
                        '</div>';
                    return;
                }
                if (status === 401) {
                    container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">' +
                        'Sign in to use watchlists.</div>';
                    return;
                }
                const lists = (body && body.watchlists) || [];
                state.watchlistsCache = lists;
                paint(lists);
            })
            .catch(err => {
                console.error('PlayerPicker watchlists error:', err);
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">Couldn\'t load watchlists.</div>';
            });
    }

    function renderWatchlistPlayers(wl) {
        const container = $('ppWatchlistsContent');
        const crumb = $('ppWatchlistsCrumb');
        // Breadcrumb: Watchlists › {name}
        crumb.style.display = 'flex';
        crumb.innerHTML =
            '<button data-back="1">Watchlists</button>' +
            '<span class="pp-breadcrumb-sep">›</span>' +
            '<button class="current">' + escapeHtml(wl.name) + '</button>';
        crumb.querySelector('[data-back]').addEventListener('click', () => {
            state.watchlistView = null;
            renderWatchlistsList();
        });

        const paint = (players) => {
            if (!players.length) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">This watchlist is empty.</div>';
                return;
            }
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-list">' + players.map(p => {
                const meta = [p.team_name || '', p.position || ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
                return playerRowHtml({
                    value: p.player_id,
                    display_name: p.name,
                    team: p.team_name || '',
                    meta: meta,
                    image_path: p.player_image,
                });
            }).join('') + '</div>';
            bindPlayerRows(container);
        };

        if (state.watchlistPlayersCache[wl.id]) { paint(state.watchlistPlayersCache[wl.id]); return; }

        container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-loading"><i class="fas fa-circle-notch"></i> Loading…</div>';
        fetch('/api/watchlists/' + wl.id + '/players?per_page=200')
            .then(r => r.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.error) {
                    container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">' + escapeHtml(data.error) + '</div>';
                    return;
                }
                const players = data.players || [];
                state.watchlistPlayersCache[wl.id] = players;
                paint(players);
            })
            .catch(err => {
                console.error('PlayerPicker watchlist players error:', err);
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="pp-empty">Couldn\'t load watchlist.</div>';
            });
    }

    // ----- Setup -----
    function init() {
        if (V2) { $('ppDrawer').classList.add('pp-v2'); $('ppScrim').classList.add('pp-v2'); }
        $('ppScrim').addEventListener('click', close);
        $('ppClose').addEventListener('click', close);
        document.querySelectorAll('.pp-tab').forEach(t => {
            t.addEventListener('click', () => switchPane(t.dataset.pane));
        });
        // Esc closes. (Cmd+K is bound by the HOST — navbar, compare, etc. — so pages
        // that already own the shortcut aren't fighting us.)
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape' && state.open) close();
        });
        wireSearch();
    }
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    } else {
        init();
    }

    // ----- Public API -----
    window.openPlayerPicker = open;
    window.closePlayerPicker = close;
    window.playerPickerIsOpen = () => state.open;
})();
</script>

            <section class="pv11-seo" aria-labelledby="pvSeoH">
    <h2 id="pvSeoH">How this page works</h2>
    <div class="pv11-seo-cols">
        <div>
            <h3>How we write a pick</h3>
            <p>One bet per match, at the price bet365 offered when we wrote it, with the case for and against. Picks are written before kick-off. Analyst members see every pick; everyone sees the free pick of the day, and every finished pick shows whether it landed.</p>
        </div>
        <div>
            <h3>How prices are checked</h3>
            <p>We check 18 bookmakers about every five minutes. Each result shows the best price on offer and how far it has moved since the first price we saw.</p>
        </div>
        <div>
            <h3>Win chance</h3>
            <p>Our model’s chance for each result, shown after the pick and the prices. It is one input, not the call.</p>
        </div>
    </div>
    <h2 class="pv11-seo-faq-h">Questions</h2>
    <dl class="pv11-seo-faq">
        
        <div><dt>How do you choose a pick?</dt><dd>We read the match: team news, form, the prices and how they moved. Then we write one bet, the price we would take at bet365, the case for it and the case against it. Every pick is written before kick-off.</dd></div>
        
        <div><dt>Where do the prices come from?</dt><dd>We check 18 bookmakers about every five minutes and show the best price for each result, with the first price we saw so you can see how it moved.</dd></div>
        
        <div><dt>Who can see the picks?</dt><dd>Everyone sees the free pick of the day and every finished pick, with whether it landed. Analyst members see every pick before kick-off, its price and the full case.</dd></div>
        
        <div><dt>What is win chance?</dt><dd>It is our model’s chance for each result before kick-off. It is one input to a pick, not the pick itself.</dd></div>
        
    </dl>
</section>

            <p class="pv11-legal">Our picks are an opinion, not betting advice. 18+. <a href="https://www.begambleaware.org" rel="nofollow noopener" target="_blank">BeGambleAware.org</a></p>
        </main>
    </div>
