<!-- Config from Jinja -->
<script>
window.__betslipConfig = {
    userStatus: 'not_logged_in',
    userIsPremium: false,
    betManagerUrl: '/bet-manager',
    loginUrl: '/login'
};
</script>

<!-- BetSlipManager IIFE -->
<script>
window.BetSlipManager = (function() {
    'use strict';

    const STORAGE_KEY = 'ss_betslip';
    const config = window.__betslipConfig || {};
    const userStatus = config.userStatus || 'not_logged_in';
    const userIsPremium = config.userIsPremium || false;

    let selectedBets = [];
    let kellySettings = { fraction: 0.5, maxStakePercent: 5, showAdvanced: true };
    let slipMode = 'singles';
    let userBankrolls = [];
    let _onBetsChanged = null;
    let SYM = '\u00a3';
    let bankrollMeta = { name: 'Bankroll', currency: 'GBP', balance: null };
    let _inited = false, _shaderMounted = false;
    const BM_URL = config.betManagerUrl || '/bet-manager';
    let lastPlaced = null;  // confirmation line shown in the slip after bets are added to Bet Manager
    const LOGIN_URL = (config.loginUrl || '/login') + '?next=' + encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname);

    function esc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])); }
    function fmtMoney(v, signed) { const n = parseFloat(v) || 0; return (n < 0 ? '-' : (signed ? '+' : '')) + SYM + Math.abs(n).toFixed(2); }
    function symFor(c) { return c === 'GBP' ? '\u00a3' : (c === 'EUR' ? '\u20ac' : '$'); }
    function toast(msg, kind) {
        const t = document.getElementById('bsToast'); if (!t) return;
        t.textContent = msg; t.className = 'bs-toast show ' + (kind || '');
        clearTimeout(toast._t); toast._t = setTimeout(() => { t.className = 'bs-toast'; }, 3200);
    }
    function emit(name, detail) { try { document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(name, { detail: detail || {} })); } catch (e) {} }
    function mountShader() {
        if (_shaderMounted) return; _shaderMounted = true;
        const host = document.getElementById('bsShader');
        if (!host || (window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches)) return;
        Promise.all([
            import('/static/js/paper-shaders/shader-mount.js'),
            import('/static/js/paper-shaders/shaders/mesh-gradient.js'),
            import('/static/js/paper-shaders/get-shader-color-from-string.js'),
            import('/static/js/paper-shaders/shader-sizing.js')
        ]).then(function (m) {
            const colors = ['#2F6AE0', '#1B3689', '#244296', '#0E1A4A', '#5B8CFF'].map(m[2].getShaderColorFromString);
            while (colors.length < 10) colors.push([0, 0, 0, 1]);
            new m[0].ShaderMount(host, m[1].meshGradientFragmentShader, {
                u_colors: colors, u_colorsCount: 5, u_distortion: 0.7, u_swirl: 0.2,
                u_grainMixer: 0.0, u_grainOverlay: 0.0, u_fit: m[3].ShaderFitOptions.cover,
                u_scale: 1.0, u_rotation: 0.0, u_offsetX: 0.0, u_offsetY: 0.0,
                u_originX: 0.5, u_originY: 0.5, u_worldWidth: 0.0, u_worldHeight: 0.0
            }, undefined, 0.16);
        }).catch(function () { /* CSS gradient stays */ });
    }

    // ── localStorage persistence ──

    function save() {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
                bets: selectedBets,
                slipMode: slipMode,
                kellySettings: kellySettings
            }));
        } catch (e) { /* quota exceeded — ignore */ }
    }

    function load() {
        try {
            const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
            if (!raw) return;
            const data = JSON.parse(raw);
            if (Array.isArray(data.bets)) selectedBets = data.bets;
            if (data.slipMode) slipMode = data.slipMode;
            if (data.kellySettings) kellySettings = { ...kellySettings, ...data.kellySettings };
        } catch (e) { /* corrupt data — ignore */ }
    }

    function pruneExpired() {
        const now = Date.now();
        const before = selectedBets.length;
        selectedBets = selectedBets.filter(b => {
            if (!b.kick_off) return true;
            return new Date(b.kick_off).getTime() > now;
        });
        if (selectedBets.length !== before) save();
    }

    // ── Text formatting helpers ──

    function norm(str) {
        return (str || '').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
    }

    function fuzzyTeamMatch(text, teamName) {
        if (!teamName || text.length < 3) return false;
        const t = norm(text);
        const tn = norm(teamName);
        return t === tn || t.includes(tn) || tn.includes(t);
    }

    function formatSelection(selection, market, homeTeam, awayTeam) {
        let s = selection || '';
        const m = (market || '').toLowerCase();

        if ((m.includes('double chance') || m.includes('match result')) && /\s+or\s+/i.test(s)) {
            const parts = s.split(/\s+or\s+/i);
            if (parts.length === 2) {
                s = parts.map((p, idx) => {
                    const t = p.trim();
                    if (/^(draw|tie)$/i.test(t)) return 'Draw';
                    if (/^home$/i.test(t)) return 'Home';
                    if (/^away$/i.test(t)) return 'Away';
                    if (fuzzyTeamMatch(t, homeTeam)) return 'Home';
                    if (fuzzyTeamMatch(t, awayTeam)) return 'Away';
                    return idx === 0 ? 'Home' : 'Away';
                }).join(' or ');
            }
        } else if (!m.includes('double chance')) {
            if (homeTeam && s.includes(homeTeam)) s = s.replace(homeTeam, 'Home');
            else if (awayTeam && s.includes(awayTeam)) s = s.replace(awayTeam, 'Away');
        }

        s = s.replace(/Clean Sheet/gi, 'CS');
        s = s.replace(/^(Home|Away)\s+CS\s+No$/i, 'No $1 CS');
        s = s.replace(/^(Home|Away)\s+CS\s+Yes$/i, '$1 CS');
        s = s.replace(/^CS\s+No$/i, 'No CS');
        s = s.replace(/^CS\s+Yes$/i, 'CS');

        if (m.includes('corner')) s = s.replace(/\s*Corners?\s*/gi, ' ').trim();
        if (m.includes('over') || m.includes('under')) s = s.replace(/\s*Goals?\s*/gi, ' ').trim();

        return s;
    }

    // ── Bankroll / Kelly helpers ──

    function getKellyStake(bet) {
        let bankroll = window.currentBankroll || 1000;
        const bankrollEl = document.getElementById('bankrollAmount');
        if (bankrollEl) {
            const v = parseFloat(bankrollEl.value);
            if (v > 0) bankroll = v;
        }
        let kellyStake = ((bet.kelly_percentage || 0) / 100) * kellySettings.fraction;
        kellyStake = Math.min(kellyStake, kellySettings.maxStakePercent / 100);
        return Math.round(bankroll * kellyStake);
    }

    async function loadBankrollFromBetManager() {
        if (userStatus === 'not_logged_in') return 1000;
        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/bet-manager/stats?t=' + Date.now(), {
                credentials: 'same-origin',
                cache: 'no-store',
                headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache', 'Pragma': 'no-cache' }
            });
            if (response.ok) {
                const stats = await response.json();
                if (stats.balance !== undefined && stats.balance !== null) {
                    const rounded = Math.round(stats.balance * 100) / 100;
                    window.currentBankroll = rounded;
                    bankrollMeta = { name: stats.bankroll_name || 'Bankroll', currency: stats.currency || 'GBP', balance: rounded };
                    SYM = symFor(bankrollMeta.currency);
                    window.currentBankrollCurrency = bankrollMeta.currency;
                    const bankrollInput = document.getElementById('bankrollAmount');
                    if (bankrollInput) bankrollInput.value = rounded;
                    renderHeader();
                    emit('betslip:bankroll', { balance: rounded, currency: bankrollMeta.currency, name: bankrollMeta.name, symbol: SYM });
                    return rounded;
                }
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error fetching bankroll from bet manager:', error);
        }
        return 1000;
    }

    async function loadUserBankrolls() {
        if (userStatus === 'not_logged_in') return;
        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/bet-manager/bankrolls', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
            if (response.ok) {
                const data = await response.json();
                userBankrolls = data.bankrolls || [];
            }
        } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
    }

    async function switchBankrollFromSlip(bankrollId) {
        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/bet-manager/bankrolls/switch', {
                method: 'POST',
                credentials: 'same-origin',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ bankroll_id: parseInt(bankrollId) })
            });
            if (response.ok) {
                await loadUserBankrolls();
                await renderPanel();
            }
        } catch (e) { console.error('Failed to switch bankroll:', e); }
    }

    // ── Panel rendering ──

    function renderHeader() {
        const v = document.getElementById('bsBankValue'), sub = document.getElementById('bsBankSub'), lbl = document.getElementById('bsBankLabel'), link = document.getElementById('bsBankLink');
        if (!v) return;
        if (userStatus === 'not_logged_in') {
            lbl.textContent = 'Track every bet';
            v.textContent = 'Free';
            sub.textContent = 'Results settle themselves in Bet Manager.';
            link.href = LOGIN_URL; link.firstChild.textContent = 'Sign in ';
            return;
        }
        lbl.textContent = bankrollMeta.name || 'Bankroll';
        v.textContent = bankrollMeta.balance == null ? '\u2014' : fmtMoney(bankrollMeta.balance);
        link.href = BM_URL;
        if (userBankrolls.length > 1) {
            sub.innerHTML = '<select class="bs-bank-select" id="slipBankrollSelect" onchange="BetSlipManager._switchBankroll(this.value)" aria-label="Bankroll">' +
                userBankrolls.map(b => '<option value="' + b.id + '"' + (b.is_default ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(b.name) + ' \u00b7 ' + symFor(b.currency) + parseFloat(b.balance || 0).toFixed(0) + '</option>').join('') + '</select>';
        } else {
            sub.textContent = 'Current balance';
        }
    }

    async function renderPanel() {
        const currentBankroll = await loadBankrollFromBetManager();
        if (userBankrolls.length === 0 && userStatus !== 'not_logged_in') await loadUserBankrolls();
        renderHeader();

        const content = document.querySelector('.bankroll-panel .bankroll-content');
        const foot = document.getElementById('bsFoot');
        if (!content) return;

        const defaultStakes = selectedBets.map(bet => {
            let ks = ((bet.kelly_percentage || 0) / 100) * kellySettings.fraction;
            ks = Math.min(ks, kellySettings.maxStakePercent / 100);
            return Math.round(currentBankroll * ks);
        });
        const n = selectedBets.length;
        const isAcca = slipMode === 'acca' && n > 1;

        if (!n && lastPlaced) {
            content.innerHTML = '<div class="bs-empty bs-placed"><span class="bs-placed-tick" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M5 12.5l4.5 4.5L19 7.5"/></svg></span><b>' + lastPlaced + '</b>Settles automatically after full time.</div>';
            foot.innerHTML = '<a class="bs-btn bs-btn--accent" href="' + BM_URL + '" onclick="try{window.ssTrack&&ssTrack(\'betslip_bm_open\',{from:\'placed\'})}catch(e){}">Open Bet Manager</a>';
            return;
        }
        if (!n) {
            content.innerHTML = '<div class="bs-empty"><b>Your slip is empty</b>Tap a price on any fixture to add it here. Bets you add settle themselves in Bet Manager.</div>';
            foot.innerHTML = userStatus === 'not_logged_in'
                ? '<a class="bs-btn bs-btn--accent" href="' + LOGIN_URL + '">Sign in to track bets</a><p class="bs-fine">Free. Automatic settlement, bankroll and full history.</p>'
                : '<a class="bs-btn bs-btn--ghost" href="' + BM_URL + '">Open Bet Manager</a>';
            return;
        }

        const rows = selectedBets.map((bet, idx) => {
            const sel = formatSelection(bet.selection, bet.market || bet.market_type, bet.home_team, bet.away_team);
            const odds = parseFloat(bet.odds) || 0;
            const prob = bet.model_prob != null ? parseFloat(bet.model_prob) : null;
            const edge = bet.edge != null ? parseFloat(bet.edge) : null;
            const chips = (prob != null && prob > 0 ? '<span class="bs-chip bs-chip--model" title="Model probability">' + prob.toFixed(1) + '%</span>' : '') +
                (edge != null && !isNaN(edge) ? '<span class="bs-chip ' + (edge >= 0 ? 'bs-chip--pos' : 'bs-chip--neg') + '" title="Edge vs market">' + (edge >= 0 ? '+' : '') + edge.toFixed(1) + '%</span>' : '') +
                (bet._source === 'acca-generator' ? '<span class="bs-chip">acca</span>' : '');
            const ko = bet.kick_off ? new Date(bet.kick_off) : null;
            const sub = esc(bet.league || '') + (ko && !isNaN(ko) ? (bet.league ? ' \u00b7 ' : '') + ko.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : '');
            return '<div class="bs-bet">' +
                '<div class="bs-bet-head"><div><div class="bs-bet-match">' + esc(bet.home_team) + ' v ' + esc(bet.away_team) + '</div><div class="bs-bet-sub">' + sub + '</div></div>' +
                '<button class="bs-remove" type="button" onclick="BetSlipManager.removeBet(' + idx + ')" aria-label="Remove"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M18 6 6 18M6 6l12 12"/></svg></button></div>' +
                '<div class="bs-bet-pick"><b>' + esc(sel) + '</b><span class="bs-odds">@ ' + odds.toFixed(2) + '</span>' + chips + '</div>' +
                '<div class="bs-stake" id="slipStake_' + idx + '"' + (isAcca ? ' style="display:none;"' : '') + '>' +
                '<span class="bs-field"><span class="cur">' + SYM + '</span><input type="number" inputmode="decimal" id="slipStakeInput_' + idx + '" value="' + defaultStakes[idx] + '" min="0" step="1" oninput="BetSlipManager._recalc()" aria-label="Stake"></span>' +
                '<span class="bs-returns" id="slipReturn_' + idx + '"></span></div>' +
                '</div>';
        }).join('');

        const accaOdds = n > 1 ? selectedBets.reduce((acc, b) => acc * parseFloat(b.odds), 1) : 0;
        const defaultAccaStake = defaultStakes.length ? Math.max(1, defaultStakes[0]) : 10;
        const modeSeg = n > 1 ? '<div class="bs-seg" role="tablist"><button type="button" class="' + (slipMode === 'singles' ? 'active' : '') + '" onclick="BetSlipManager._setMode(\'singles\')">Singles</button><button type="button" class="' + (slipMode === 'acca' ? 'active' : '') + '" onclick="BetSlipManager._setMode(\'acca\')">' + n + '-fold acca</button></div>' : '';
        const acca = '<div class="bs-acca" id="accaSection" style="display:' + (isAcca ? 'grid' : 'none') + ';">' +
            '<div><div class="k">Combined odds</div><div class="v num">' + accaOdds.toFixed(2) + '</div></div>' +
            '<div><div class="k">Stake</div><span class="bs-field" style="margin-top:3px;"><span class="cur">' + SYM + '</span><input type="number" inputmode="decimal" id="accaStakeInput" value="' + defaultAccaStake + '" min="0" step="1" oninput="BetSlipManager._recalc()" aria-label="Acca stake"></span></div>' +
            '<div><div class="k">Returns</div><div class="v num pos" id="accaReturn">' + fmtMoney(defaultAccaStake * accaOdds) + '</div></div></div>';
        const kelly = '<details class="bs-kelly" id="bsKelly"' + (kellySettings.showAdvanced ? ' open' : '') + ' ontoggle="BetSlipManager._toggleAdvanced(this.open)">' +
            '<summary>Stakes from Kelly <span class="info" onclick="event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); if (typeof openKellyInfo === \'function\') openKellyInfo();">What is this?</span><svg class="chev" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9"/></svg></summary>' +
            '<div class="bs-kelly-body">' +
            '<label>Fraction<select id="kellyStrategy" onchange="BetSlipManager._updateKelly()"><option value="0.25"' + (kellySettings.fraction === 0.25 ? ' selected' : '') + '>Quarter Kelly</option><option value="0.5"' + (kellySettings.fraction === 0.5 ? ' selected' : '') + '>Half Kelly</option><option value="1"' + (kellySettings.fraction === 1 ? ' selected' : '') + '>Full Kelly</option></select></label>' +
            '<label>Max stake, % of bankroll<span class="bs-field"><input type="number" id="maxStakePercent" value="' + kellySettings.maxStakePercent + '" min="1" max="100" oninput="BetSlipManager._updateKelly()"></span></label>' +
            '<label>Bankroll used for sizing<span class="bs-field"><span class="cur">' + SYM + '</span><input type="number" id="bankrollAmount" value="' + (Math.round(currentBankroll * 100) / 100) + '" oninput="BetSlipManager._syncBankroll(this)"></span></label>' +
            '<label>&nbsp;<button type="button" class="bs-btn bs-btn--ghost" style="min-height:34px;font-size:12px;" onclick="BetSlipManager._refreshBankroll()">Reset to live balance</button></label>' +
            '<div class="bs-kelly-note">Suggested stakes come from the model probability and the odds. They are a sizing reference, not advice.</div>' +
            '</div></details>';

        content.innerHTML = modeSeg + rows + acca + kelly;

        const cta = userStatus === 'not_logged_in'
            ? '<a class="bs-btn bs-btn--accent" href="' + LOGIN_URL + '">Sign in to track ' + (n === 1 ? 'this bet' : 'these bets') + '</a><p class="bs-fine">Free. Your selections stay in the slip while you sign in.</p>'
            : '<button type="button" class="bs-btn bs-btn--accent" id="placeBetsBtn" onclick="BetSlipManager._placeBets()">Add ' + (isAcca ? 'acca' : (n === 1 ? 'bet' : n + ' bets')) + ' to Bet Manager</button>' +
              '<div class="bs-actions"><button type="button" class="bs-btn bs-btn--ghost" onclick="BetSlipManager.clear()">Clear slip</button><button type="button" class="bs-btn bs-btn--ghost" onclick="BetSlipManager._exportCSV()">Export CSV</button></div>';
        foot.innerHTML = '<div class="bs-totals"><span>Total stake <b id="bsTotStake">\u2014</b></span><span class="ret">Returns <b id="bsTotReturn">\u2014</b></span></div>' + cta;
        recalcSlipSummary();
    }

    // ── UI update helpers ──

    function updateCounterUI() {
        const count = selectedBets.length;
        const counter = document.getElementById('betCounter');
        const counterTop = document.getElementById('betCounterTop');
        if (counter) { counter.textContent = count; counter.style.display = count > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none'; }
        if (counterTop) { counterTop.textContent = count; counterTop.style.display = count > 0 ? 'inline-block' : 'none'; }
        const head = document.getElementById('bsHeadCount');
        if (head) head.textContent = count ? (count + ' selection' + (count === 1 ? '' : 's')) : '';
        const dock = document.getElementById('bsDock');
        if (dock) {
            const open = document.getElementById('bankrollPanel').classList.contains('active');
            dock.classList.toggle('is-visible', count > 0 && !open);
            const t = document.getElementById('bsDockTitle'), s = document.getElementById('bsDockSub');
            if (t) t.textContent = count + ' selection' + (count === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' in your slip';
            const db = document.getElementById('bsDockBtn');
            if (db) db.textContent = (slipMode === 'acca' && count > 1) ? 'Track acca' : (count === 1 ? 'Track bet' : 'Track ' + count + ' bets');
            if (s) {
                const acc = count > 1 ? selectedBets.reduce((a, b) => a * (parseFloat(b.odds) || 1), 1) : (parseFloat((selectedBets[0] || {}).odds) || 0);
                s.textContent = count > 1 ? (count + '-fold pays ' + acc.toFixed(2)) : (count === 1 ? selectedBets[0].home_team + ' v ' + selectedBets[0].away_team + ' @ ' + acc.toFixed(2) : '');
            }
        }
        emit('betslip:changed', { count: count });
    }

    function notifyChanged() {
        updateCounterUI();
        save();
        if (typeof _onBetsChanged === 'function') _onBetsChanged();
    }

    // ── Staking ──

    function recalcSlipSummary() {
        let totStake = 0, totReturn = 0;
        if (slipMode === 'acca' && selectedBets.length > 1) {
            const combinedOdds = selectedBets.reduce((acc, b) => acc * parseFloat(b.odds), 1);
            const accaStakeEl = document.getElementById('accaStakeInput');
            const stake = accaStakeEl ? parseFloat(accaStakeEl.value) || 0 : 0;
            const accaReturn = document.getElementById('accaReturn');
            if (accaReturn) accaReturn.textContent = fmtMoney(stake * combinedOdds);
            totStake = stake; totReturn = stake * combinedOdds;
        } else {
            selectedBets.forEach((bet, idx) => {
                const input = document.getElementById(`slipStakeInput_${idx}`);
                const stake = input ? parseFloat(input.value) || 0 : 0;
                const returnEl = document.getElementById(`slipReturn_${idx}`);
                if (returnEl) returnEl.innerHTML = stake > 0 ? 'returns <b>' + fmtMoney(stake * parseFloat(bet.odds)) + '</b>' : 'enter a stake';
                totStake += stake; totReturn += stake * parseFloat(bet.odds);
            });
        }
        const ts = document.getElementById('bsTotStake'), tr = document.getElementById('bsTotReturn');
        if (ts) ts.textContent = fmtMoney(totStake);
        if (tr) tr.textContent = fmtMoney(totReturn);
    }

    function applyKellyStakes() {
        let bankroll = window.currentBankroll || 1000;
        const bankrollEl = document.getElementById('bankrollAmount');
        if (bankrollEl) {
            const v = parseFloat(bankrollEl.value);
            if (v > 0) bankroll = v;
        }

        selectedBets.forEach((bet, idx) => {
            let ks = ((bet.kelly_percentage || 0) / 100) * kellySettings.fraction;
            ks = Math.min(ks, kellySettings.maxStakePercent / 100);
            const stakeAmount = Math.round(bankroll * ks);
            const input = document.getElementById(`slipStakeInput_${idx}`);
            if (input) input.value = stakeAmount;
        });

        if (selectedBets.length > 0) {
            const avgKelly = selectedBets.reduce((sum, b) => sum + (b.kelly_percentage || 0), 0) / selectedBets.length;
            let accaKellyStake = (avgKelly / 100) * kellySettings.fraction;
            accaKellyStake = Math.min(accaKellyStake, kellySettings.maxStakePercent / 100);
            const accaStakeAmount = Math.round(bankroll * accaKellyStake);
            const accaInput = document.getElementById('accaStakeInput');
            if (accaInput) accaInput.value = accaStakeAmount;
        }

        recalcSlipSummary();
    }

    // ── Bet placement ──

    async function placeBets() {
        if (userStatus === 'not_logged_in') {
            window.location.href = '/login?next=' + encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname);
            return;
        }
        if (selectedBets.length === 0) return;

        // Validate stakes before anything is sent: one zero stake would
        // otherwise post as a partial failure.
        if (slipMode === 'acca' && selectedBets.length > 1) {
            const v = parseFloat((document.getElementById('accaStakeInput') || {}).value);
            if (!(v > 0)) { toast('Enter a stake for the acca', 'bad'); return; }
        } else {
            for (let i = 0; i < selectedBets.length; i++) {
                const el = document.getElementById(`slipStakeInput_${i}`);
                const v = el ? parseFloat(el.value) : getKellyStake(selectedBets[i]);
                if (!(v > 0)) { toast('Enter a stake for every selection', 'bad'); if (el) el.focus(); return; }
            }
        }

        const placeBetsBtn = document.getElementById('placeBetsBtn');
        if (placeBetsBtn) {
            placeBetsBtn.disabled = true;
            placeBetsBtn.textContent = 'Adding';
        }

        let successCount = 0;
        let failCount = 0;

        if (slipMode === 'acca' && selectedBets.length > 1) {
            let stakeAmount = 10;
            const accaInput = document.getElementById('accaStakeInput');
            if (accaInput) {
                const v = parseFloat(accaInput.value);
                if (v > 0) stakeAmount = v;
            }
            const combinedOdds = selectedBets.reduce((acc, b) => acc * parseFloat(b.odds), 1);

            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/bets/place-acca', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    credentials: 'same-origin',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        legs: selectedBets.map(b => ({
                            fixture_id: b.fixture_id,
                            home_team: b.home_team,
                            away_team: b.away_team,
                            league: b.league || '',
                            market_type: b.market || b.market_type || '',
                            selection: b.selection,
                            odds: parseFloat(b.odds),
                            model_probability: b.model_prob || 0,
                            edge: b.edge || 0,
                            kick_off: b.kick_off || new Date().toISOString()
                        })),
                        combined_odds: Math.round(combinedOdds * 100) / 100,
                        stake: stakeAmount,
                        notes: `${selectedBets.length}-fold acca`
                    })
                });
                if (response.ok) successCount = selectedBets.length;
                else failCount = selectedBets.length;
            } catch (error) {
                failCount = selectedBets.length;
            }
        } else {
            for (let i = 0; i < selectedBets.length; i++) {
                const bet = selectedBets[i];
                let stakeAmount = getKellyStake(bet);
                const stakeInput = document.getElementById(`slipStakeInput_${i}`);
                if (stakeInput) {
                    const v = parseFloat(stakeInput.value);
                    if (v > 0) stakeAmount = v;
                }

                try {
                    const response = await fetch('/api/bets/place', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        credentials: 'same-origin',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            fixture_id: bet.fixture_id,
                            home_team: bet.home_team,
                            away_team: bet.away_team,
                            league: bet.league,
                            market_type: bet.market || bet.market_type,
                            selection: bet.selection,
                            odds: parseFloat(bet.odds),
                            model_probability: bet.model_prob || 0,
                            edge: bet.edge || 0,
                            kelly_percentage: bet.kelly_percentage || 0,
                            stake: stakeAmount,
                            kick_off: bet.kick_off
                        })
                    });
                    if (response.ok) successCount++;
                    else failCount++;
                } catch (error) {
                    failCount++;
                }
            }
        }

        if (failCount > 0) {
            toast(successCount + ' added, ' + failCount + ' failed. Check the stake and try again.', 'bad');
        } else if (successCount > 0) {
            const msg = slipMode === 'acca' && successCount > 1
                ? successCount + '-fold acca added to Bet Manager'
                : successCount + (successCount === 1 ? ' bet' : ' bets') + ' added to Bet Manager';
            toast(msg, 'ok');
            lastPlaced = msg;
            selectedBets = [];
            notifyChanged();
            await renderPanel();
            emit('betslip:placed', { count: successCount });
        }

        if (placeBetsBtn) {
            placeBetsBtn.disabled = false;
            placeBetsBtn.textContent = 'Add to Bet Manager';
        }
    }

    function exportCSV() {
        if (selectedBets.length === 0) return;

        let csvContent = "Match,Market,Selection,Odds,Edge %,Stake,Potential Return\n";
        selectedBets.forEach((bet, idx) => {
            let stakeAmount = getKellyStake(bet);
            const stakeEl = document.getElementById(`slipStakeInput_${idx}`);
            if (stakeEl) {
                const v = parseFloat(stakeEl.value);
                if (v > 0) stakeAmount = v;
            }
            const potentialReturn = stakeAmount * parseFloat(bet.odds);
            csvContent += `"${bet.home_team} vs ${bet.away_team}","${bet.market || bet.market_type || ''}","${bet.selection}",${parseFloat(bet.odds).toFixed(2)},${bet.edge || 0},${stakeAmount.toFixed(2)},${potentialReturn.toFixed(2)}\n`;
        });

        const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv' });
        const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = `bet_slip_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        document.body.removeChild(a);
    }

    // ── Public API ──

    return {
        init: function(options) {
            options = options || {};
            if (options.onBetsChanged) _onBetsChanged = options.onBetsChanged;
            if (_inited) { updateCounterUI(); return; }
            _inited = true;
            load();
            pruneExpired();
            updateCounterUI();
            renderHeader();
            // Bankroll straight away: the sidebar card and the toolbar read it
            // before the slip is ever opened.
            loadBankrollFromBetManager().then(() => { renderPanel(); });
        },

        clear: function() {
            if (!selectedBets.length) return;
            selectedBets = [];
            notifyChanged();
            renderPanel();
        },

        addBet: function(betObj) {
            // Deduplicate by fixture_id + selection
            const fid = betObj.fixture_id;
            const sel = betObj.selection;
            if (fid && sel && selectedBets.some(b => b.fixture_id === fid && b.selection === sel)) {
                return false; // already exists
            }
            selectedBets.push(betObj);
            lastPlaced = null;
            notifyChanged();

            // Auto-render if panel is open
            if (document.getElementById('bankrollPanel').classList.contains('active')) {
                renderPanel();
            }
            return true;
        },

        removeBet: function(idx) {
            if (idx >= 0 && idx < selectedBets.length) {
                selectedBets.splice(idx, 1);
                notifyChanged();
                renderPanel();
            }
        },

        addAccaLegs: function(legs, source) {
            let added = 0;
            legs.forEach(leg => {
                const fid = leg.fixture_id;
                const sel = leg.selection;
                if (fid && sel && selectedBets.some(b => b.fixture_id === fid && b.selection === sel)) {
                    return; // skip duplicate
                }
                // Calculate kelly for each leg
                const modelProb = parseFloat(leg.model_prob || leg.probability || 0) / 100;
                const odds = parseFloat(leg.odds);
                const kellyPct = odds > 1 ? Math.min(((modelProb * odds - 1) / (odds - 1)) * 100, 25) : 0;

                selectedBets.push({
                    fixture_id: leg.fixture_id,
                    home_team: leg.home_team,
                    away_team: leg.away_team,
                    league: leg.league || leg.league_name || '',
                    market: leg.market || leg.market_type || '',
                    market_type: leg.market || leg.market_type || '',
                    selection: leg.selection,
                    odds: odds,
                    model_prob: leg.model_prob || leg.probability || 0,
                    edge: leg.edge || 0,
                    kelly_percentage: kellyPct,
                    kick_off: leg.kick_off || new Date().toISOString(),
                    _source: source || 'unknown'
                });
                added++;
            });

            if (added > 0) {
                // Auto-switch to acca mode when multiple legs added
                if (selectedBets.length > 1) {
                    slipMode = 'acca';
                }
                notifyChanged();
                // Auto-open the panel
                const panel = document.getElementById('bankrollPanel');
                const backdrop = document.getElementById('panelBackdrop');
                panel.classList.add('active');
                panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
                if (backdrop) {
                    backdrop.classList.add('active');
                    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                }
                mountShader();
                updateCounterUI();
                renderPanel();
            }
            return added;
        },

        toggle: async function() {
            const panel = document.getElementById('bankrollPanel');
            const backdrop = document.getElementById('panelBackdrop');
            const open = !panel.classList.contains('active');
            panel.classList.toggle('active', open);
            panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', open ? 'false' : 'true');
            if (backdrop) {
                backdrop.classList.toggle('active', open);
                document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
            }
            updateCounterUI();
            if (open) { mountShader(); await renderPanel(); }
        },

        open: function() { const p = document.getElementById('bankrollPanel'); if (!p.classList.contains('active')) return this.toggle(); },

        close: function() {
            const panel = document.getElementById('bankrollPanel');
            const backdrop = document.getElementById('panelBackdrop');
            panel.classList.remove('active');
            panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
            if (backdrop) backdrop.classList.remove('active');
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
            updateCounterUI();
        },

        hasBet: function(fixtureId, selection) {
            return selectedBets.some(b => b.fixture_id === fixtureId && b.selection === selection);
        },

        get selectedBets() {
            return selectedBets;
        },

        // Internal helpers exposed for onclick handlers
        _recalc: recalcSlipSummary,
        _setMode: function(mode) {
            slipMode = mode;
            save();
            renderPanel();
        },
        _toggleAdvanced: function(open) {
            kellySettings.showAdvanced = !!open;
            save();
        },
        _updateKelly: function() {
            const strategyEl = document.getElementById('kellyStrategy');
            const maxEl = document.getElementById('maxStakePercent');
            if (strategyEl) kellySettings.fraction = parseFloat(strategyEl.value);
            if (maxEl) kellySettings.maxStakePercent = parseFloat(maxEl.value) || 5;
            save();
            applyKellyStakes();
        },
        _syncBankroll: function(sourceEl) {
            const v = parseFloat(sourceEl.value);
            if (v > 0) {
                window.currentBankroll = v;
                applyKellyStakes();
            }
        },
        _refreshBankroll: async function() {
            const bankroll = await loadBankrollFromBetManager();
            const bankrollInput = document.getElementById('bankrollAmount');
            if (bankrollInput) bankrollInput.value = Math.round(bankroll * 100) / 100;
            applyKellyStakes();
        },
        _switchBankroll: switchBankrollFromSlip,
        _placeBets: placeBets,
        _exportCSV: exportCSV,

        // Expose formatSelection for page code that needs it
        formatSelection: formatSelection
    };
})();

/* Pages that never called init() (the v9 predictions board) still get a
   persisted slip, a live bankroll and the dock. */
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    if (window.BetSlipManager) BetSlipManager.init({});
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && window.BetSlipManager) {
        var p = document.getElementById('bankrollPanel');
        if (p && p.classList.contains('active')) BetSlipManager.close();
    }
});
</script>

<script>
(function() {
    function detectAndApplyDarkMode() {
        const nav = document.getElementById('mobileBottomNav');
        if (!nav) return;

        const styles = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
        const bgBase = styles.getPropertyValue('--bg-base').trim();
        const bgPrimary = styles.getPropertyValue('--bg-primary').trim();

        if (bgBase === '#000000' || bgBase === '#000' ||
            bgPrimary === '#000000' || bgPrimary === '#000') {
            nav.classList.add('dark-mode');
            return;
        }

        const bodyBg = window.getComputedStyle(document.body).backgroundColor;
        const match = bodyBg.match(/\d+/g);
        if (match) {
            const [r, g, b] = match.map(Number);
            if ((0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b) < 50) {
                nav.classList.add('dark-mode');
            }
        }
    }

    function stabilizeNavPosition() {
        const nav = document.getElementById('mobileBottomNav');
        if (!nav) return;
        nav.style.display = 'none';
        nav.offsetHeight;
        nav.style.display = '';
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', detectAndApplyDarkMode);
    } else {
        setTimeout(detectAndApplyDarkMode, 0);
    }

    let resizeTimeout;
    window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
        clearTimeout(resizeTimeout);
        resizeTimeout = setTimeout(stabilizeNavPosition, 100);
    }, { passive: true });

    const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
    document.querySelectorAll('.mobile-nav-item').forEach(item => {
        const page = item.getAttribute('data-page');
        const href = item.getAttribute('href');

        if (currentPath === href ||
            (page === 'home' && /^(\/es)?\/dashboard\/?$/.test(currentPath)) ||
            (page === 'scout-hub' && currentPath.startsWith('/scout-hub')) ||
            (page === 'predictions' && currentPath.includes('/predictions/match')) ||
            (page === 'fpl' && currentPath.startsWith('/fpl')) ||
            (page === 'player-props' && currentPath.includes('/player-props')) ||
            (page === 'fixtures' && currentPath.startsWith('/fixtures'))) {
            item.classList.add('active');
        }
    });
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
    var bar = document.getElementById('ssIosBar'); if (!bar) return;
    var forced = /[?&]iosbar=1\b/.test(location.search), ua = navigator.userAgent || '';
    var browser = forced ? 'test'
        : !/iPhone|iPod|iPad/.test(ua) || navigator.standalone ? ''
        : /CriOS/.test(ua) ? 'chrome' : /FxiOS/.test(ua) ? 'firefox' : /EdgiOS/.test(ua) ? 'edge'
        : /OPiOS|OPT\//.test(ua) ? 'opera' : /GSA\//.test(ua) ? 'google-app' : /DuckDuckGo/.test(ua) ? 'duckduckgo'
        : /FBAN|FBAV|FB_IAB/.test(ua) ? 'facebook' : /Instagram/.test(ua) ? 'instagram' : /Snapchat/.test(ua) ? 'snapchat'
        : /TikTok|musical_ly|BytedanceWebview/.test(ua) ? 'tiktok' : /Twitter/.test(ua) ? 'x' : /LinkedInApp/.test(ua) ? 'linkedin'
        : /Version\/[\d.]+.*Safari\//.test(ua) ? 'safari' : 'webview';
    // Not an iPhone, or Safari: Apple's Smart App Banner covers Safari.
    if (!browser || browser === 'safari') return;
    var KEY = 'ssib_hide_until';
    if (!forced) { try { if (+localStorage.getItem(KEY) > Date.now()) return; } catch (e) {} }
    var page = bar.getAttribute('data-page');
    function t(n) { if (forced) return; try { if (window.ssTrack) window.ssTrack(n, { page: page, browser: browser }); } catch (e) {} }
    function hide() { bar.hidden = true; document.body.classList.remove('ssib-on'); }
    setTimeout(function () {
        bar.hidden = false; document.body.classList.add('ssib-on');
        void bar.offsetWidth;  // commit the start position so the slide-up runs
        bar.classList.add('on');
        t('ios_bar_view');
    }, 1200);
    // Same tab, straight to the App Store app (19 Oct 2026). A target=_blank link
    // opened a new tab that iOS Chrome and the Google app closed again, which looked
    // like the page reloading. itms-apps opens the store from those browsers and from
    // in-app browsers; the https link is the fallback if the store did not open.
    document.getElementById('ssIosGet').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        t('ios_bar_click');
        if (forced) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        var href = this.href;
        location.href = 'itms-apps://apps.apple.com/app/id6753829935';
        setTimeout(function () { if (!document.hidden) location.href = href; }, 1500);
    });
    document.getElementById('ssIosX').addEventListener('click', function () {
        try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, String(Date.now() + 14 * 864e5)); } catch (e) {}
        t('ios_bar_dismiss'); hide();
    });
})();
</script>
